| Derived   |                        | Extending all the      |               |
|           |                        | segments gives a       |               |
|           |                        | common intersection on |               |
|           |                        | the left. Their        |               |
|           |                        | extensions lack a      |               |
|           |                        | common intersection on |               |
|           |                        | the right.             |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+

## BP081

+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+
| Version   | Image                  | Rule                   | Link          |

| Foundalis | [![BP081F][img/BP081F] | “Dark figures can be   | [BP081F       |

|           |                        | figures by a straight  |               |
|           |                        | line alternatively:    |               |
|           |                        | Convex hulls of filled |               |
|           |                        | and outline figures    |               |
|           |                        | are disjoint; Dark     |               |
|           |                        | figures are impossible |               |
|           |                        | to separate from the   |               |
|           |                        | outline ones           |               |
|           |                        | alternatively: Convex  |               |
|           |                        | hulls of filled and    |               |
|           |                        | outline figures        |               |
|           |                        | overlap”               |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+
| OEBP      | [![BP081E][img/BP081E] | “Dark figures can be   | [BP081E       |
|           | ][img/BP081E]          | divided from outline   | ][url/BP081E] |
|           |                        | figures by a straight  |               |
|           |                        | line vs. convex hulls  |               |
|           |                        | of filled and outlined |               |
|           |                        | figures overlap.”      |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+
| Derived   |                        | A straight line can    |               |
|           |                        | separate the filled    |               |
|           |                        | shapes from the hollow |               |
|           |                        | shapes on the left.    |               |
|           |                        | Their convex hulls     |               |
|           |                        | overlap on the right.  |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+

|           |                        | point leftward ("wind" |               |
|           |                        | from right)”           |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+
| Derived   |                        | The stem and branches  |               |
|           |                        | sweep toward the right |               |
|           |                        | in the left examples   |               |
|           |                        | and toward the left in |               |
|           |                        | the right examples.    |               |
+-----------+------------------------+------------------------+---------------+
